公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開
曼群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專
業生產各類氣動器材的集團 ， 公司主要從事
生產氣動控制元件 、 氣動執行原件 、 氣源處
理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售等業
務，以 「AirTAC」 自有品牌銷售於大中華地
區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台灣佔 2％、
海外佔 4％ ， 現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動
元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC 在中國市佔
率排名第一，達 33％。

主要競爭對手：SMC、Festo

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

亞德客-KY (1590 TT) 
保持成長趨勢

永豐觀點 
10 月公司日出貨量仍優於前幾個月，4Q26 營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與 3Q26
持平，AI 擴散效應 2027 年將持續發酵，建議逢低買進，區間操作。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議：理由包括：(1) 10 月公司日出貨量仍優於前幾個月，10 月應用領
域中仍有多個領域表現好 ， 這當中應有AI 擴散效應帶來電子業成長的貢獻 。4Q26
營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與 3Q26 持平，(2) 2026 全年維持先前營收成長高於
20%的看法，營業利益率則調高至 35%，氣動元件 2026、2027 年仍處於上升週
期，預估公司營收皆將保持雙位數成長，(3) 公司 2026 年 payout 比例 65%，預
計 2027 年將再提高。評價方面，公司本益比區間 20-40 倍，目前僅 16 倍，處於
相對低檔，AI 擴散效應持續發酵，2026、2027 年獲利在成長軌道上，建議逢低買
進，區間操作，目標價維持 1972 元（27X 2027EPS）。

ESG 評析 
亞德客企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

亞德客為中國第二大氣動元件廠商：亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開曼
群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集團， 公司主要從
事生產氣動控制元件、 氣動執行原件、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售
等業務，以「AirTAC」自有品牌銷售於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台灣
佔 2％、海外佔4％，現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。 
3Q26 EPS 達 14.23 元，符合預期：亞德客 3Q26 營收 115.12 億元 （+39.61%），
營業利益率 33.92%，稅後淨利 28.47 億元（+35.59%YoY），EPS 為 14.23 元，
符合預期。目前中國金屬原物料成本仍在高檔，以加第三季調漲薪資，不過在公司
降本增效的措施持續實施下，第三季營業利益率優於預期。3Q26 有效稅率拉升，
乃因公司有意增加 2027 年以後的 payout 比例 ， 從中國匯出獲利將加徵10%的稅
率。目前氣動元件稼動率高於 100%、線軌稼動率高於 40%，存貨水準 110 天相
對較低（130-140 天屬健康水準） 。 
2026 年全年營收將成長 20%以上，2027 年主要應用領域亦有雙位數成長：過去
氣動元件 2 年一個上升週期，2 年一個下降週期，本波 2025 年開始進入上升週
期，趨勢較溫和，預計成長可超過 2-3 年。2022-2024 年行業雙位數衰退，2025
低個位數成長、預估 2026 將高個位數成長，而 2027 年中位數成長。2026 年以
來公司每月出貨與訂單量皆優於預期 ， 與電子業AI 擴散效應 、 公司規格持續擴增 、
市占率提升等因素有關。行業應用別當中，電子業 3Q26 營收成長 33%，電子業
中 25%-30%與智慧手機有關，主要客人在產品上市前3-6 個月拉貨，預估客戶一
般規格新手機明年上半年上市，故公司 3Q26 手機應用領域成長 26%YoY，預估
可延續至第四季，今年與明年電子行業預估依舊可成長雙位數，此乃因為 AI 擴散
效應將延續；電池行業中國政府有調控政策，9 月公司應收下滑 10%YoY， 不過10
月又恢復 15%-20%的成長，故未來成長幅度仍需觀察，不過中國廠商移至國外擴
廠，對行業成長性仍是有助益；汽車行業公司市占率僅 10%-20%，在市占率持續
提升下，今年與明年雙位數成長；傳統行業如食品、包裝、工具機等受惠於政府補
貼政策，客戶積極擴充自動化產能，以及廠商回到中國擴廠，預估今年與明年依舊
雙位數成長；半導體受惠於中國國產化政策，過去每月營收 500 萬元人民幣，目
前已達 1300-1400 萬元，2027 年新規格上市，4Q26 可讓客戶開始認證，公司規
劃十年後年營收可達 10 億元人民幣。 
線軌市場市況仍在好轉中 ：線軌 2025 出貨量成長 25%，3Q26 營收成長 27%YoY，
今年市場傳動元件市場景氣回升 ， 螺桿大幅調漲價格 ， 線軌景氣也好轉 、 交期拉長 ，
但價格漲幅並不大。公司線軌營收也隨市場水漲船高，目前稼動率 40%（去年
20%+） ，預估年底時可達50%，屆時毛利率可達 30%，稼動率 80%時，毛利率
40%。公司規劃十年後年營收可達 30 億元人民幣。 
電控產品已開始貢獻營收：電控系統中國市場規模 1000 億元人民幣，但有很多不
同系統，公司會挑市場規模大的產品跨入，該產品資本支出低但毛利率高。過去幾
年利用五個系列產品在去年時營收達 4 億元人民幣，主要是搭配氣缸銷售傳感器，

營運現況與分析

3

今年將再推出 3 個系列，今年以來營收成長 20%以上。公司預估未來 10 年公司
有機會做到 30 億元人民幣年營收。 
10 月日出貨金額持續 MoM 成長、4Q26 預計營收可 QoQ 持平：10 月公司日出
貨量仍優於前幾個月，但因工作天數較少，營收不一定會高於 9 月，10 月應用領
域中仍有多個領域表現好 ， 這當中應有AI 擴散效應帶來電子業成長的貢獻 。4Q26
營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與3Q26 持平，2026 全年維持先前營收成長高於 20%
的看法，營業利益率則調高至 35%（先前為 34%） 。至於2027 年，前述氣動元件
主要應用領域皆呈現雙位數的成長，線軌成長預計可超過 20%，在線軌稼動率提
升、原物料平穩下，預計營業利益仍可維持高檔。 
財務預估：預估 2026 年合併營收為 451.46 億元(+31.5％YoY)，稅後淨利 118.13
億元(+40.6％YoY)，EPS 為 59.07 元 。 預估2027 年合併營收為 533.99 億元(+18.28
％YoY)，稅後淨利 143.44 億元(+21.42％YoY)，EPS 為 71.72 元。

## Document 039: 20261104【投研報告】市場快訊_美期中選舉牽動市場表現，股債配置重品質、控風險

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261104【投研報告】市場快訊_美期中選舉牽動市場表現，股債配置重品質、控風險.pdf

美期中選舉牽動市場表現，股債配置重品質、控風險
2026 年 11 月 04 日
一、 市場動態
1. 美期中選舉牽動市場情緒，亞股一度隨美股期貨下挫：美國 11 月 3 日舉行期中選舉
，根據 CNN 網站的結果顯示，截至台北時間 11/4 下午 3 點左右，民主黨於參議院
已取得 100 席中的 53 席的過半席次，於眾議院則在 435 席中取得了 225 席(218 席
過半)。市場重新評估國會控制權及川普政府未來政策推動能力，擔心民主黨國會影響
力擴大 ，可能對財政政策及科技企業監管形成新的制衡 。S&P 500 期貨與 Nasdaq 100

期貨在開票期間一度下挫 1.38%及 2.35%，科技股明顯承受較大賣壓。惟隨後跌深反
彈，截至台北時間下午 3 時左右，S&P 500 期貨與 Nasdaq 100 期貨跌幅已收斂至
0.22%及 0.44%。亞洲市場方面，科技股為主及出口導向股市受到美股期貨拖累，台
灣加權指數 11/4 盤中最低下挫 3.38%，所幸隨跌幅收斂，終場下跌 0.83%；韓股同
日也由大跌近 4%由黑翻紅上漲 0.62%。11/3 未開盤的日股，在 11/4 開盤後出現補
跌，最低下挫近 4.8%，但最後跌幅收斂至下跌 1.42%。
2. 美國公債及美元同步反映政策預期調整，市場避險情緒升溫：美國公債及美元反映政
策預期調整，殖利率盤中先跌後漲：10 年期公債殖利率盤中先跌後漲，截至台北時間
下午 3 時來到 4.906%；同一時間，美元指數小跌約 0.37%。股、債、匯市的變動顯
示，投資人除了調整股票風險部位，也在評估未來美國財政刺激規模、政府債務供給
與利率前景。
二、 下跌原因
1. 民主黨取得國會控制權，市場重新定價財政與監管政策：本次選舉對金融市場的主要

影響，在於未來行政與立法部門的權力分配可能改變。共和黨先前同時掌握白宮與國
會，有利於推動稅制、財政及產業政策；民主黨在參眾兩院均取得過半席次後，部分

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
政策將面臨更高的協商成本。財政方面，新增減稅及擴大支出的空間可能受到限制，
市場因而下修部分財政刺激預期；企業方面，國會監督權可能增加對大型科技企業、
AI 資料中心、反壟斷及資料隱私議題的調查壓力。惟國會控制權改變不代表既有政策
立即逆轉，也不代表所有監管措施均能完成立法。此次市場反應更可能是投資人重新
要求政策風險溢酬，而非企業獲利已發生實質下修。
2. AI 資本支出與投資回報疑慮尚未消退，放大科技股對政治事件的反應：此次選舉開票
期間，Nasdaq 期貨及與科技相關的亞股出現大幅波動，亦與近期科技股基本面及評

價爭議有關。10 月底以來美股因大型科技企業 AI 投資效益受到質疑而大幅修正，市
場關注焦點逐漸由 AI 基礎建設支出規模 ， 轉向相關投資能否創造足夠的營運現金流與
股東報酬。以雲端服務及資料中心產業而言，企業雖可透過AI 運算需求增加帶動營收
成長，但 GPU、伺服器、電力設備及資料中心建設需要龐大前期資金，若營收回收速
度不如預期，可能使自由現金流承壓並增加外部融資需求。近期部分科技企業籌資計
畫及財測不佳，已使投資人提高對財務品質的要求。當市場原先已對AI 投資回報存有
疑慮，新的政治及監管不確定性便更容易放大評價修正幅度。
三、 市場看法
1. 半導體需求仍具分化 ， 政治事件不等於AI 產業成長趨勢反轉 ：科技股近期的股價修正
與 AI 終端需求惡化並非相同概念。AMD 近期公布優於市場預期的財報，並對資料中
心業務提出較強勁的成長展望，顯示 AI 運算需求仍具有一定韌性；相對而言，Intel
先前財測疲弱及現金流壓力，則反映不同半導體企業的產品競爭力、資本效率及終端
市場曝險存在明顯差異。因此，不宜將半導體股整體下跌解讀為AI 需求全面降溫。從

產業鏈觀察，具備技術領先優勢、穩定客戶需求及良好現金流的企業，與高度依賴外
部融資、尚未形成穩定獲利模式的基礎建設業者，對資金成本及監管變化的承受能力
並不相同。未來市場可能更加重視個別企業的獲利品質，而非單純以AI 相關營收成長
作為估值依據。
2. 政治事件可能延長科技股評價調整，但中期走勢仍取決於企業獲利：本次期中選舉引
發的期貨跌勢，反映市場對政策方向及國會控制權變化的即時反應，惟政治不確定性
通常須透過實際立法、企業投資計畫或經濟活動變化，才會形成較持久的基本面影響
。從企業獲利角度觀察，路透社引用 LSEG 資料指出，S&P 500 第三季企業獲利仍呈
現強勁成長，科技企業對整體獲利貢獻顯著，顯示目前尚無充分證據支持美國企業基
本面全面轉弱。不過，科技股先前已累積較高成長預期，市場對資本支出回報及監管
風險的容忍度降低，可能使股價即使在營收成長下仍承受評價壓力。短期而言，若選
舉結果逐漸明朗，且企業獲利預估未進一步下修，市場有機會逐步消化政治風險；反
之，若政策不確定性伴隨獲利預期下修，修正時間可能延長。

### Page 3

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. 歷史經驗顯示選後股市多正向，後續表現仍宜留意基本面變化：根據彭博統計，1950
年以來美國歷次期中選舉後 12 個月，S&P 500 平均報酬率約 15%，且 19 次全數
收正。其中最高為 1954 年的逾 30%，表現最弱的 1986 年亦小幅上漲。我們認為
，選前政策不確定性消除加上府會分治抑制財政與監管的轉向是為歷史上選後行情的
主要支撐，但相關數據所呈現的為過往經驗，未來股市表現能否延續先前情況仍應觀
察後續基本面變化。
4. 美國公債具避險價值，但利率下行能否持續仍取決於通膨與財政前景：在美國期中選

舉開票期間，美股期貨下跌伴隨美債殖利率回落，顯示公債仍能在股市波動期間提供
一定的風險分散效果。然而，本次殖利率下降可能同時包含避險買盤、財政刺激預期
下修及市場對經濟成長前景重新評估等因素，不宜直接解讀為聯準會即將轉向寬鬆。
尤其能源市場仍受到中東地緣政治及供應中斷風險影響，油價波動可能使通膨前景反
覆，限制長天期公債殖利率的下行空間。若未來財政擴張受到限制，可能對長端利率
形成部分支撐；但若政治僵局提高債務上限協商風險，亦可能使美債期限溢酬波動。
因此，債券配置仍須同時考量收益率、存續期間與通膨風險，不宜僅根據單日行情決
定配置方向。
5. 信用市場是判斷風險是否由股權評價擴散至金融條件的重要指標：目前科技股修正主
要反映評價與政策風險，但若AI 企業持續增加資本支出及外部融資，市場對債務負擔
與償債能力的關注可能進一步升高。對大型投資等級科技企業而言，較穩定的營運現
金流及融資管道有助於吸收資本支出壓力；但對依賴單一客戶合約、設備抵押融資或
高槓桿經營的 AI 基礎建設業者 ， 利率與信用利差上升可能更直接影響投資回報及再融

資能力。後續應追蹤投資等級債與高收益債 OAS、科技及雲端相關發行人利差、新債
發行需求及信用評等展望。若科技股下跌但整體信用利差維持穩定，市場風險可能仍
以股權評價調整為主；若信用利差同步持續擴大，則須提高對金融條件收緊及風險跨
市場擴散的警覺。

### Page 4

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
四、 投資建議：
1. 科技股中期成長趨勢仍具支撐，惟短線宜審慎布局並重視個股分化：美國期中選舉帶
來的政策不確定性，短期可能使科技股評價承壓，惟大型雲端業者持續投入AI 基礎建
設，半導體及相關設備需求仍具中期成長動能，目前尚不宜因美股期貨在開票期間的
下挫即全面轉向保守。我們仍建議維持美股核心配置，但短期避免過度集中於高評價
、高資本支出及高度依賴外部融資的企業。產業方面，資訊科技維持選擇性配置，優
先關注具備技術競爭優勢、穩定營運現金流及較高投資回報能見度的企業；對雲端基
礎建設及 AI 融資相關標的，則應提高財務結構與現金流審查標準。

2. 維持中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債配置，兼顧收益與風險分散：在美國政策風
險升溫及科技股評價調整期間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源
價格的不確定性，現階段不建議延長存續期間。建議以中天期美國公債及高信評投資
等級債作為主要配置方向，兼顧票息收益、利率敏感度與資產配置穩定性。信用債方
面，建議優先選擇營運現金流充足、槓桿適中及再融資需求可控的發行人，對AI 相關
企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合約現金流與債務到期結構。

上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。

1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。

交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

聯發科 (2454 TT)  Trading Buy

客戶與專案將於 1Q28 貢獻營收，投資建議給 Trading Buy 。

QoQ+14.58%\YoY+10.80% ；稅後 EPS 17.37 元。  
4Q26 、 2026 全年展望優於預期： 財務指引：假設匯率為 31.7 ，
聯發科估 4Q26 營收， 2,101~2,218 億台幣， QoQ+25~32% ，毛利
率 45±1.5% 、費用率 26±2% 。 2026 全年營收將優於先前預期的
美元營收高個位數成長，主因為 AI 加速器業務放量與漲價。  
AI ASIC 業務的營益率高於公司平均： AI ASIC 業務的毛利率
低於公司平均，但從 4Q26 開始，對營益率有正向幫助， AI ASIC
業務的營益率高於公司平均，但由於其他業務有漲價，所以無
法量化差異。  
由於 4Q26 開始，營益率因 AI ASIC 業務已經開始改善，加上
相關營收將於 2027 年放量，聯發科認為 2027 年營益率至少能
在 20% 以上。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 2 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
環境保護：聯發科技做為全球半導體產業技術領導者，在整體價值鏈中位於
最前端的晶片設計，後端的原物料採購、代工製造、封裝與測試等環節皆交
由供應商執行，形成專業分工與互相合作的夥伴關係。根據風險調查與碳足
跡盤查發現，主要對社會與環境產生衝擊影響的關鍵在於後端製造作業，故
如何與供應商共同合作，以降低社會與環境相關的潛在風險，為聯發科技發
展綠色營運的首要任務。  
社會責任：運用數位科技解決社會問題不是企業的專利，聯發科技相信每個
人都有潛力利用數位科技創造無限可能，聯發科發起數位社會創新競賽 「智
在家鄉」，鼓勵社會大眾為家鄉問題提供解方。  
公司治理：聯發科技董事會下設有審計委員會，強化公司治理獨立性，並設
立薪資報酬委員會，以健全公司薪酬制度為目標，持續強化企業永續發展。
除前述法定功能性委員會外，本公司亦設立法定以外之併購策略委員會，視
實際需求針對集團具體併購案件及整體併購策略評估分析。  
3Q26 產品組合與說明：  
手機晶片占比 37% ，營收 QoQ-1%\YoY-17% ，因搭載 2nm 的天璣 9600 Pro
晶片的手機陸續上市，旗艦級產品表現優於其他產品。  
智慧裝置平台占比 58%，營收 QoQ+20%\YoY+63% ，因 5G 數據晶片導入平
板、 PC、車用座艙等裝置，並且 TV 晶片反映 DRAM 價格。天璣 9500 已搭
載於三星平板。  
PMIC 占比 5%，營收 QoQ+1%\YoY+18% ，因資料中心相關市占率提升。  
AI ASIC 業務的營益率高於公司平均：  
AI ASIC 業務的毛利率低於公司平均，但從 4Q26 開始，對營益率有正向幫
助， AI ASIC 業務的營益率高於公司平均，但由於其他業務有漲價，所以無
法量化差異。  
由於 4Q26 開始，營益率因 AI ASIC 業務已經開始改善，加上相關營收將於
2027 年放量，聯發科認為 2027 年營益率至少能在 20% 以上。  
聯發科第二個 AI ASIC 客戶與專案將於 2028 年開始貢獻營收：  
聯發科原先估第一個 AI ASIC 貢獻 4Q26 營收約 20 億美元，現在上修至貢
獻 25 億美元， 2027 年目標貢獻 160 億美元營收，為 AI ASIC 市場約 20% 市
佔。  
第二個 AI 加速器 ASIC 專案將於 2028 年初量產，此為新的非中系客戶，第
二個專案採用 Multi-die ，一些 die 已經 taped-out ，一些較大顆、較複雜的
die 會晚數周，仍符合時程。  
由於第二個 AI 加速器是採用 Multi-die ，合作對象為美國 IDM 與基板供應
商，聯發科會與台灣、日本供應商一起合作，由於供應鏈還有些變數，聯發
科暫時無法給出 2028 年相關業務的更多細節，目前只表示是非中系客戶。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 156: 261103_ms_PC-semis

Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Idea
Greater China  Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
PC Semis: More divergent
outlooks
Business diversification, KGD (known good die) pricing power
and supply chain management create a divergent revenue
outlook for PC semis into 4Q and likely also 1Q27. Downgrade
Elan to EW.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
Elan Microelectronics Corp
(2458.TW) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$250.00 NT$160.00
A much tougher 4Q… We mentioned in the prior PC semis note that the FOMP
(fear of missing procurement) could lead to  stable PC semis shipments in 3Q; now,

the inventory accumulation in 1Q-3Q26 could cause a deeper correction in 4Q.  PC
semis customers currently still aim for only a 5-10% Q/Q procurement decline in 4Q.
That said, the downside surprise in Parade’s 4Q outlook (link), which calls for a 20-
25% Q/Q revenue decline, could suggest a worse component correction. The still-
elevated Windows activation period of 200+ days and much smaller price hikes
from PC semis customers in 2H vs. 1H could suggest ongoing weakness into 1Q27.

But why are some companies resilient? Many investors are asking why Realtek’s
revenue could be up Q/Q in 4Q amid the PC correction (link). We cite three factors:
1) Consumer demand (other than PC/smartphones) is not necessarily all bad,
especially for IoT devices (low memory content). Espressif is guiding for  Q/Q
revenue growth in 4Q, following sequential growth in 3Q. 2) Price hikes reflect
increases at both  foundries/OSATs and in KGD. For example, TV SoC pricing is

several times higher than in past years due to  KGD price hikes (mainly DDR4). 3)
Large-scale IC design houses usually have better supply chain management and
lower exposure to PC.
No new growth driver into 2027: With the recent debate on cloud AI, we think it’s
still a matter of time before edge AI  takes off. However, we haven’t seen a clear
growth driver for PC semis. Preliminary outlooks from PC semis customers range
from flattish to down 20% Y/Y in 2027. Thus, it's  difficult to turn positive now.

Reduce earnings: We revise down Elan's PT and earnings and cut the stock to EW.
We appreciate the company's growth from smart Edge AI devices, but its exposure
to NB is still too significant to overlook. Our PT implies 16x our downward-revised
2027 EPS. Stay EW  Realtek and UW  Novatek, ASMedia, Parade, and Nuvoton.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
The FOMP (fear of missing procurement for PC semis in 1Q-3Q26) has caused a
deeper correction for some PC semis into 4Q.
It's easier for large-scale PC semis  to navigate through the downturn, while small
design houses are more concentrated on PC.
We stay conservative on PC semis, with no OW and await the cycle bottom

sometime in 2027.
November 3, 2026 09:00 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Elan Micro: Earnings estimate revisions
We revise our EPS estimates by +1%/-20%/-16% for 2026/27/28: For 2026, we
incorporate actual 2Q26 results, which benefited from investment gains. For 2027-28, we
reduce our revenue and GM forecasts due to continued weak PC demand, which we
believe cannot be fully offset by the non PC business (mainly the drone market).
Exhibit 1: Elan: Estimate revisions
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates.

Exhibit 2: Elan: Quarterly financial statement
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates
Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$160: This reflects our earnings estimate revisions for
2026-28. Our price target is our base-case scenario value, which we continue to derive
from our residual income model. We reduce our intermediate growth rate estimate from
10.0% to 8.0% to reflect a weak PC business outlook. We reduce our terminal growth rate

estimate from 4.5% to 3.0% to better align it with broader economic growth. We continue
to assume a cost of equity of 9.6% and a payout ratio of 80%.
Our bull case value is cut to NT$234 from NT$366, and our bear case value is cut to NT
$84 from NT$131.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Winbond Electronics Corp  | Asia Pacific
Strong 3Q margins; growth to
moderate in 4Q
3Q26 earnings beat: 3Q revenue was NT$82bn, +38% Q/Q, +278% Y/Y, 8% below
MSe; GM was 70.6%, +4.3ppt Q/Q, +23.9ppt Y/Y, 12.5ppt above MSe; EPS was NT
$7.49, +39% Q/Q, +1045% Y/Y, 3% above MSe. CMS revenue grew 50% Q/Q as ASP
increased by ~60%, despite lower bit shipments, which was due to reserving supply
for L TA customers. Flash revenue rose 34% Q/Q on pricing and mix with flat bit

shipments. Memory GM reached 73.9%.
4Q growth to moderate; 16nm supports 2027: Management expects continued 4Q
growth, but a slower pace as non-AI demand softens. DRAM contract prices should
rise more moderately than in 3Q, while high density NOR remains tight. KH module
A capacity should reach 24-25kwpm by end-2026. 16nm ramp from ~2kwpm to
16kwpm is expected to support 2H27 shipments.
AI opportunities broaden; module  B capex approved: Management projects server

NOR bit shipments to grow 67% Y/Y in 2027. CUBE customers are increasingly
inference accelerator developers with several expected to begin small volume
production in 2027. Silicon capacitor capacity is largely allocated, but current
margins remain below conventional DRAM. The board newly approved NT$73bn
capex for the previously announced Module B expansion.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)
Base case, 2.9x 2027e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside, a sustainable NOR price hike, and opportunities in SLC NAND and SiCap
foundry business.

Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand
n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 211: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-04

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
90 
SK하이닉스  (000660) 
2026.11.04

HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

업황 우려에 고유 할인까지 겹친 과도한 하락 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 230만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27 년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다[표1]. 이는 상장 이후 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 해당한다. 
‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 102%, 66%, 47%로, ‘26년 정점 이후에도 과거 업사이클 
고점보다 높은 수준이 유지될 전망이다.  
한편, 이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해져 시장의 시야가 ‘28년으로 향할 경우 
‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 360만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 57% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -51%를 넘어섰고, 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
그러나 당사는 아래와 같은 투자포인트와 향후 주가 흐름을 제시한다.

‘28년 HBM 가치, 시가총액의 1.3배 
HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 cHBM으로 
갈수록 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 
물량과 가격이 결정된다. 동사는 이 전환이 업계에서 가장 선두권이다.  
당사는 동사 HBM 매출이 ‘26년 43조원에서 ‘28년 183조원으로 증가할 것으로 
전망한다. 만약, 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E(2 1배, 
15배, 1 2배)를 적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 47%, 93%, 135%를 
설명한다[표3]. ‘27 년에 이미 HBM 하나만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명된다는 
의미다. 시장이 통상적으로 받아들이는 멀티플 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 
다르지 않다.

150만원에 사면 ‘30년 EPS 2배 
동사의 주주환원은 자사주 매입/소각 중심일 것으로 상정한다. 당사는 동사가 현 
정책 기간(‘25~’27년)에 이어 ‘28년 이후에도 FCF의 50%를 환원하고 이 중 
70%를 자사주 매입/소각에 쓸 것으로 가정했다.  
당사 가정대로라면 총주주환원수익률은 현 시가총액 대비 ‘27년 14%, ‘28~’30년 
연 17~24%다[표4]. 
관건은 매입 단가다. 자사주 매입의 주당가치 개선 효과는 P/E가 낮을수록 
커지는데, 현 주가의 ‘27년 P/E는 2.7배다. 현 주가 수준인 150만원에 매년 균등 
매입한다고 ‘30년까지 주식수는 50% 줄어든다.   
그 결과 ‘30년 주당순이익은 매입이 없을 때 대비 2배가 된다[표4]. ‘28년 이후 
이익이 정체되더라도 주당 이익은 계속 늘어나는 구조이며, 이익 지속성에 대한 
의심이 주가를 누를수록 소각 효과는 커진다. 현 주가 수준의 정체가 장기화되기 
어려운 이유다.

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간이다. 솔리다임 상장 철회에 이어 3분기 
실적발표에서 ‘26년 주주환원 100조원과 매 분기 자사주 매입 방침이 제시되면서 환원 
의지는 검증되었다. 
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 불확실성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 가격 하락폭은 LTA 
방어력이 확인되기 전까지 블랙박스다. 이 불확실성이 멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 
계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 구간이 이어질 전망이다. 이 구간의 
기회비용은 환원으로 보상받는다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 신규 팹 물량이 소량 
출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 당사는 2H27에 ‘28년 HBM 협의와 
LTA 연장이 지속적으로 확인되면서 이익이 스트레스 테스트 범위 안에서 유지될 것으로 
전망하며[그림2], 이것이 숫자로 확인되는 시점에 순환 할인이 축소되고 재평가가 나타날 
것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.

 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

## Document 251: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름삼성바이오로직스(207940) 환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름환율 하락에도 견조한 흐름 - 기업

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

## Document 296: A股10月复盘与11月展望——全球AI信用重定价下的调整，与政策维稳下的磨底期

Source PDF: all report pdf/A股10月复盘与11月展望——全球AI信用重定价下的调整，与政策维稳下的磨底期.pdf
Report source: 东吴证券
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 23
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告·宏观报告·宏观深度报告

东吴证券研究所

A股10月复盘与11月展望——全球AI信
用重定价下的调整，与政策维稳下的磨底期

2026年11月04日

证券分析师  芦哲 
执业证书：S0600524110003 
 luzhe@dwzq.com.cn 
证券分析师  唐遥衎 
执业证书：S0600524120016 
 tangyk@dwzq.com.cn

相关研究  
《如何理解央行外汇存款准备金率调
升？》 
2026-11-04 
《反常识和消费不等式：谁决定消费
增速？》 
2026-11-03

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 投资要点 
◼ 10月行情的本质：AI信用重定价与内部去拥挤共振： 
◼ 2026年10月万得全A回调12.44%，为2016年1月以来第三大单月跌
幅，呈“上旬下行、中旬弱势、下旬加速”三段式，收于月内低点。与
9月不同，10月美债10年期收益率下行约35.6个基点至4.929%，A股
却加速回调，主导矛盾已从“无风险利率”切换为“AI叙事信用审视+
高拥挤、高杠杆筹码被动去化”。全月日均成交16097亿元、缩量12.1%，
两融余额降1833亿元，属存量资金被动降杠杆，而非恐慌出逃。 
◼ 行业分化：防御与能源相对占优，AI链全面重定价： 
◼ 2026年10月行业分化程度较高，31个申万一级行业中仅5个正收益，
且全部集中于“防御+能源”：食品饮料+5.31%、银行+4.84%、石油石
化+3.34%、煤炭+1.29%、家电+0.56%；红利指数+2.33%。领跌为通信-
30.23%、电子-25.70%、建材-17.96%、综合-17.31%、电力设备-15.88%。
电子1—9月利润同比增1.0倍而股价单月回调25.70%，“业绩向上、
股价向下”表明10月调整可能更多反映估值重估与持仓结构变化，而
非仅由当期盈利表现解释。 
◼ 宏观背景：外部AI信用冲击，内部政策与数据拉锯： 
◼ 2026年10月，海外AI定价从“资本开支扩张=增长”切换为“回报与
信用风险审视”：英特尔单日回调28.58%，亚马逊800亿美元供应商贷
款引发融资结构担忧，存储芯片指数全月回调约24.8%，费城半导体回
调超过15%，纳指回调5.30%，标普500仅回调0.93%。通胀黏性压缩
转松空间：美国9月CPI同比3.7%、PCE同比3.6%，布伦特原油涨
13.6%；10月FOMC维持3.75%—4.00%不变但两名票委主张加息。国
内总量平稳、结构分化，10月制造业PMI回落至49.3%；五中全会后
金融管理部门密集释放“稳市场”信号。 
◼ 市场核心分歧解析： 
◼ 核心分歧集中于六问：10月市场回调更宜理解为“AI信用重定价+内部
筹码去化”的叠加影响；美债收益率回落与A股调整同期出现，说明单
一利率因素对当月走势的解释力有限。政策维稳有助于稳定预期、降低
极端波动风险，后续企稳仍需政策支持、基本面预期与交易结构改善共
同验证，短期或震荡修复，中期仍待信号确认，快速V型反转偏乐观情
形。双创风险度处于历史低位，赔率有所提升；但回调幅度本身并非反
弹持续性的充分条件，胜率提升还需“AI信用冲击缓和+成交承接改善”
双重确认。防御红利空间有限，同时需关注交易拥挤度上升后的性价比
变化。微盘、北证去拥挤在量能层面已较充分，趋势层面仍需跟踪确认。
11月日历效应可作为有限但可参考的季节性线索。 
◼ 11月展望： 
◼ 2026年11月权益综合信号虽转为-2，但万得全A风险度处于2.7%较低
分位，短期下行空间受限、超跌修复概率上升；基准情景由‘震荡磨底’
调整为‘短期震荡修复、中期磨底确认’。历史-2样本偏谨慎，但当前
低风险度与政策维稳密集，短期不宜简单套用历史均值。 
◼ 核心结论与总结 
◼ 2026年10月核心是“全球AI信用重定价”与“内部拥挤端去化”共振
深调，而非国内基本面恶化或海外利率上行；月末政策维稳信号密集，
但指数收于月内低点，政策底信号与市场底筹码仍有时滞。11月是“政
策维稳+超跌约束”与“外部风险窗口+AI信用冲击”的拉锯，基准为短
期震荡修复，中期持续性看‘成交回升+AI链企稳’双确认。结构上短期
超跌成长弹性更大，防御/能源可作底仓；微盘与北证在增量工具落地后
弹性有望改善。 
◼ 风险提示：美国中期选举后地缘升级；美联储12月加息；AI信用冲击
延续；去杠杆负反馈；政策维稳不及预期；数据口径风险。整体需关注
海外利率、地缘冲突、微盘拥挤与政策兑现

1.   2026年10月行情复盘：一场"不是利率"的调整 
1.1.   传导闭环：为何利率回落未能带动A股同步修复 
 2026年10月A股的回调，拆开看是一条与9月方向相反的四步传导链： 
【叙事端】全球AI信用重定价（英特尔盈利指引不及预期、存储链回调、科技巨
头资本开支与现金流压力） → 【结构端】A股AI硬件链（电子/通信）深度回调→双
创指数失去核心支撑 → 【资金端】高融资余额下的被动降杠杆（两融余额月内大幅回
落、成交缩量）→ 拥挤端（双创/微盘/北证）集中去化 → 【风格端】仅存防御与能源
正收益，市场风险偏好回落，正收益行业范围收窄。 
 这条链的每一环都有数据支撑： 
【叙事端】：2026年10月全球市场对AI的定价逻辑从"资本开支扩张=增长"切换
到"资本开支扩张=回报与信用风险"。标志性事件包括：2026年10月30日英特尔股价
单日回调28.58%（第四季度盈利指引不及市场预期）、亚马逊披露800亿美元供应商贷
款额度引发"AI融资结构"担忧；10月28日SK海力士公布三季度营业利润同比暴增7
倍并启动大规模股东回报后，股价当日上涨10%，但存储链整体在"涨价兑现"与"需求透
支"之间剧烈摆动（存储芯片指数全月回调约24.8%）。"业绩好"与"股价回调"同时出现，
是本轮AI重定价的核心特征——市场开始为"AI资本开支的融资成本与投资回报"定价，
而非为"景气度"定价。 
【结构端】：A股AI硬件链（通信-30.23%、电子-25.70%）的回调幅度远大于其基
本面变化（2026年1-9月电子行业利润同比增长1.0倍，拉动全部规上工业企业利润增
长9.6个百分点），说明10月下的主导变量是估值与筹码，而非业绩。 
【资金端】：全月日均成交16097亿元、环比缩量12.1%；两市融资余额由9月末
25324亿元降至10月29日23492亿元（-1833亿元）——这是2026年以来两融余额幅
度最大的一段回撤，融资盘的被动减持与双创/微盘的回调形成负反馈。 
【风格端】：申万一级行业中仅食品饮料（+5.31%）、银行（+4.84%）、石油石化
（+3.34%）、煤炭（+1.29%）、家用电器（+0.56%）录得正收益，红利指数上涨2.33%、
风险度位于自成立以来66.3%分位；而双创（创业板指-23.02%/科创50-24.13%）、微盘
（-14.74%）、北证50（-14.80%）等拥挤端全面去化。 
根源是"定价锚"的切换：9月A股的下跌是"贴现率上行带来的估值压力"（美债逼
近5.4%），10月美债回落但A股加速下跌，说明当前市场的主导矛盾已从"无风险利率
"切换为"AI叙事的信用与回报审视+内部筹码结构"。隐含的关键推论是：用"美债利率
阈值"（如跌破5.0%）作为A股修复条件在10月被证伪，11月需要观察的是AI信用冲
击的减弱信号（费半企稳、半导体信用利差收敛）与内部杠杆去化的进程（两融余额止
跌）。

### Page 5

5 / 23 
2026年10月深调源于"AI信用重定价+内部去拥挤"的共振，而非国内基本面或海
外利率；这意味着修复路径也不同于前两轮——先等信用冲击减弱与杠杆去化完成，再
谈基本面与政策的重新定价。

1.2.   三段式走势：全月下行，月末收于月内低点 
2026年10月万得全A的下跌呈清晰的"三段式"结构（数据截至2026年10月30
日）：

表1：2026年10月万得全A三段式走势（数据区间2026-09-30至2026-10-30） 
阶段 区间 涨跌 特征 
上旬下行 2026年10月8日 -10月14日 -4.41% 节后调整，AI相关板块开始 走弱 
中旬弱势 2026年 10月 15 日-10月 23日 -2.71% 油价通胀与中选不确定性 压制， 10 月 22 日单日 - 2.45% 
下旬加速 2026年 10月 26 日-10月 30日 -5.85% 10月 27日-2.23%、10月 29 日-2.13%、10 月 30 日- 2.34%，收于月内低点 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所 
三段式之中，单日较大回调主要出现在以下四个交易日：2026年 10月 22日（-
2.45%）、10月30日（-2.34%）、10月15日（-2.28%）、10月27日（-2.23%）；反
弹仅两个交易日相对显著：10月23日（+2.02%）与10月28日（+1.55%）。月内最高
收盘6107.00点（10月9日）、最低收盘5425.00点（10月30日），全月收于月内最
低、且当日未见明显的护盘承接。 
这一走势结构提示两个关键信息：其一，10月的回调不是"单日恐慌宣泄"（不同于
9月月末的两根大阴线），而是全月三个阶段连续走弱，外生催化（AI信用、油价、中
选）与内生结构（高融资、高拥挤）持续共振，属于典型的"趋势性去化"而非"情绪性错
杀"；其二，月末创月内新低意味着下跌的动能尚未自然衰竭，11月上旬的修复更可能
来自"外力"（政策维稳、外围风险释放）而非"自发的超跌反抽"。

三点观察：其一，"越大越抗跌"取代了9月的"越小越抗跌"——上证 50（-2.88%）
与红利（+2.33%）成为仅有的低波品种，而2026年9月表现最强的微盘、双创、北证

大面积落入历史低位——万得全A风险度-1.17（2.7%分位）、创业板指风险度-6.06（1.5%
分位）、科创50风险度-7.42（1.8%分位），大盘成长类资产的"超跌程度"超过2024年
初与2022年10月的水平；其三，防御端拥挤度已不低——红利指数风险度74.29（66.3%
分位）、微盘股风险度自9月末的106.1回落至73.9（48.9%分位），防御方向的"低位
安全垫"正在变薄，而拥挤端经过一个月的去化已回到历史中位附近——这为11月的结
构判断提供了重要前提。

2.   行业分化：防御与能源相对占优，AI链全面重定价 
2026年10月行业表现的分化程度明显：31个申万一级行业中仅5个录得正收益，
且全部集中于"防御+能源"；跌幅前三的通信、电子、建筑材料均超过17%，AI硬件链
（通信/电子）与内需周期链（建材/机械/地产）形成了两个主要承压方向。

归因上有一个容易被忽略的背离：2026年1-9月电子行业利润同比增长1.0倍（国

下跌25.70%——"业绩向上、股价向下"的背离，表明10月调整可能更多反映估值重估
与持仓结构变化，而非仅由当期盈利表现解释。这一背离对11月的含义是双面的：一
方面，跌深之后的"业绩确定性"标的具备修复弹性基础；另一方面，只要AI叙事层面的

### Page 8

8 / 23 
信用冲击（资本开支融资、回报审视）未减弱，业绩兑现就难以单独支撑股价——修复
需要"叙事企稳"与"业绩兑现"两者同向。 
三季报的另一面：银行（农业银行单季净利润同比+10.84%、六大行前三季合计净
赚约1.14万亿元）、有色金属（矿企与算力金属企业业绩亮眼）、部分高端制造（源杰
科技前三季归母净利润同比+866.88%、中际旭创+211.81%）的业绩兑现度较高，但股价
表现出现分层——"业绩兑现但筹码拥挤"的品种在去杠杆阶段被优先减持，这是 10月
与以往财报季最大的不同。 
3.   宏观背景：外部"AI信用冲击"，内部"政策与数据的拉锯" 
3.1.   海外：AI叙事重定价、通胀黏性与中选变数 
10月最大的海外变化是市场对AI产业前景的定价逻辑发生变化。 2026年10月
中下旬起，美股对AI的定价从"资本开支扩张→景气上行"转向"资本开支扩张→融资成
本与回报审视"：2026年10月30日英特尔股价单日下跌28.58%（四季度指引不及预
期）、高通下跌11.12%、AMD下跌10.88%；亚马逊因披露800亿美元供应商贷款额度
引发"AI债务融资结构"担忧（此前还出现英特尔、亚马逊信用违约互换报价跳升的报道，
规模级别约123.5个基点）；存储链先涨后跌，存储芯片指数全月下跌约24.8%。这轮
重定价的直接后果是美股涨跌结构剧烈分化：标普500单月仅下跌0.93%，但纳指下跌
5.30%、费城半导体指数下跌逾15%，而标普500等权重指数上涨4.94%——"部分指数
调整相对有限、板块表现分化"是10月全球权益市场的共同特征。 
通胀黏性叠加地缘溢价，压缩了货币政策转松的空间。 2026年10月14日公布的
美国9月CPI同比上涨3.7%（高于预期3.6%、前值3.4%）、核心CPI同比2.6%（高
于预期）；10月30日公布的美国9月PCE同比3.6%（5月以来最高）、核心PCE同
比升至3.1%；同期布伦特原油单月上涨13.6%（收于117.64美元/桶、地缘风险溢价主
导）。油价→通胀→利率预期的链条使"美联储转松预期"进一步后延：10月28日FOMC
（美东时间）维持联邦基金利率目标区间3.75%-4.00%不变，库克与哈马克两名票委主
张加息25个基点，会后声明未对12月是否加息给出明确信号；达拉斯联储主席Logan
（2026年票委）会后表示"仍预计至少需要再加息两次、但可循序渐进"。"暂停但未转
向"是本次会议的核心信息。 
美国中期选举与地缘风险构成11月的最大不确定性组合。 美国中期选举将于2026
年11月3日投票，选前民调与库克政治报告均显示民主党形势改善；据多方报道，美
方已通报海湾盟友准备在选举后（最快11月4日）恢复对伊朗的"短时高强度打击"，卡
塔尔等调解方在10月30日-31日于德黑兰、多哈进行密集斡旋（报道口径）。"选举落
地前的观望+选举后的地缘窗口"是 11月海外风险的两段式结构，对应油价的风险溢价
路径：选举前以降温交易为主（10月下旬油价曾单日回落逾2%）、选举后若打击落地
则溢价再度上冲。

3.2.   国内：数据"总量平稳、结构分化"，政策密集维稳 
三季度经济：总量平稳、投资弱、出口强。 2026年10月19日国家统计局公布三
季度数据：前三季度GDP同比增长4.6%（总量107.11万亿元），三季度单季增长4.5%、
环比回升0.2个百分点；9月规模以上工业增加值同比+5.5%（工业机器人、集成电路产
量增长均超18%）、社会消费品零售总额同比+1.1%（剔除汽车拖累后+3.9%）；但1-9
月城镇固定资产投资同比下降7.2%、地产开发投资同比下降20.6%（新建商品房销售额
同比-13.9%），投资端是总量数据中最弱的一环。物价方面：9月CPI同比+0.9%（核心
CPI+1.0%）、PPI同比+5.0%（2022年6月以来最高，其中汽油价格同比+16.6%）——
PPI高读数由能源与输入性因素主导，"再通胀的交易属性"强于"需求属性"。9月城镇调
查失业率5.2%、较上月回落0.1个百分点。 
金融与利润：直接融资占比抬升、工业利润保持两位数增长。 2026年10月14日
央行公布前三季度金融数据：社会融资规模增量累计27.46万亿元（同比少 2.63万亿
元）、9月末社融存量同比+7.1%、M2同比+7.2%、前三季度人民币贷款增加11.52万亿
元；结构上债券与股票融资在社融增量中占比约49.5%、超过贷款占比约7个百分点。

### Page 10

10 / 23 
2026年 10月 27日公布的工业利润数据显示：1-9月规模以上工业企业利润同比增长
15.5%（利润总额60337.4亿元）、9月单月同比+14.4%（今年6月以来最高），其中电
子行业利润同比增长1.0倍、高技术制造业利润增长52.0%。"金融数据总量收敛、结构
向直接融资倾斜"与"工业利润由AI链与能源链拉动"并存，是10月基本面数据的主线。 
政策端：从"金融街论坛"到"五中全会"的密集维稳信号。 政策线索按时间排序：
①2026年10月19日-21日金融街论坛年会召开，央行行长潘功胜表示将推动退出存贷
款基准利率、完善"DR001+贷款市场报价利率"的双定价基准体系；②10月26日-29日
党的二十届五中全会在北京召开（研究持之以恒推进全面从严治党若干重大问题、审议
通过《决定》，10月30日举行新闻发布会解读）；③10月30日央行、证监会、金融
监管总局等金融管理部门分别召开会议传达全会精神，其中证监会明确"全力稳市场稳
预期，深化投融资综合改革，完善全链条监管体系，持续提升资本市场韧性和信心"；
④10月30日央行公告公开市场操作"换锚"：自11月2日起每个工作日开展隔夜逆回
购操作招标（操作利率1.25%），7天期逆回购调整为固定数量、利率招标、多重价位
中标并主要在税期、月末等时点开展；⑤全国人大常委会第二十五次会议将于11月2
日-6日举行，金融法草案将提请二次审议（拟加强对资产管理产品与服务的统一规范、
跨境金融活动监管）。"稳市场"表述与操作层面的"换锚"是10月末政策端最重要的边际
变化，其含义是短端利率调控框架向价格型进一步转型、并为跨月流动性提供新的工具
安排。 
10月31日公布的中国10月PMI回落至收缩区间： 制造业PMI 49.3%（前值50.1%）、
非制造业商务活动指数49.7%、综合PMI产出指数与两项分项同向回落——PMI重回荣
枯线下方，与9月数据的"温和改善"形成反差，也为11月政策加码提供了依据。

4.   市场核心分歧解析 
分歧一：这轮下跌的主因是"海外利率"还是"AI叙事+内部结构"？ 市场在 10月
仍习惯用"美债利率"解释A股，但数据不支持：10月美债10年期收益率下行了约35.6
个基点至4.929%，A股却加速下跌。我们的回答是后者：下跌的主因是"AI叙事的信用
重定价"与"高拥挤、高杠杆筹码的被动去化"，海外利率10月反而是"逆风减弱"的。这
一判断的可检验推论是：若11月A股企稳，应首先看到费半企稳、两融余额止跌与AI
链缩量收敛， 而非美债进一步下行——用利率阈值跟踪A股修复条件在10月已被证伪。 
分歧二：政策维稳信号能否让市场"见底"？ 10月末维稳信号密度为2026年最高
（五中全会+证监会“全力稳市场稳预期”+央行换锚+金融法二审），政策信号主要作用
于预期与流动性，而“政策底”向“市场底”的过渡通常还需要筹码出清（融资盘、拥挤端）
与外部风险释放（中选、地缘）形成共振。10月30日指数收于月内低点，量价特征显
示筹码出清仍在推进，完成度需后续数据进一步验证。基准判断是：政策维稳有助于降
低尾部风险、稳定预期与流动性环境；趋势扭转则需要更多条件配合。见底形态更可能
经历缩量整固和反复确认，V型反转可作为乐观情形之一。 
分歧三：超跌成长（双创）能否凭借"跌得多"反弹？ 创业板指风险度分位已降至
1.5%（自成立以来）、科创50风险度分位降至1.8%，“超跌”程度接近或超过历史较低
分位，赔率有所提升。但10月的一个重要证据是：跌幅本身不构成反弹持续性的充分

### Page 12

12 / 23 
条件——本轮回调的传导源（AI信用冲击）仍在全球层面消化（英特尔、亚马逊等事
件），筹码层面（融资盘、主题基金、部分量化产品）的出清进度也需要从价格之外的
多维度确认。10月下旬以来双创曾出现多次单日反抽（10月23日、10月28日），显
示一定弹性，但连续性仍需成交与信用信号配合。判断：双创具备“赔率”，而“胜率”的
确认需要两个条件——AI信用冲击减弱（费半止跌+相关信用指标收敛）与成交结构改
善（缩量止跌或放量承接）；两者出现前，反弹持续性宜按逐步验证处理，关注技术性
反抽向趋势修复转化的条件。 
分歧四：防御与红利还能不能追？ 10月红利指数上涨2.33%、银行上涨4.84%，
防御与能源是当月主要的正收益方向；但防御的拥挤度已经抬升（红利指数风险度74.29、
处自成立以来66.3%分位），且银行业具有"高位放量滞涨"的微观特征（工商银行月内
创历史新高后于月末走弱）。我们的回答：防御依然是基准情景下的相对占优方向，但
其"相对收益属性"强于"绝对收益空间"——在油价与利率的再通胀组合下，防御的性价
比依赖"风险偏好继续收缩"这一前提，该前提若被政策维稳与中选落地削弱，防御的进
攻性会快速衰减。 
分歧五：微盘/北证/双创的"去拥挤"结束了吗？ 10月微盘股风险度由 106.1回落
至73.9（自历史98.2%分位回落至48.9%分位）、短期动量降至9.1，北证50月内回调
14.80%、收于月内低点。拥挤度指标已明显回落，调整进程进入“后半程”，但结束与否
仍需观察；两市融资余额10月下降1833亿元，月末企稳信号仍需跟踪。判断：去拥挤
已进入后半程，短期继续大幅去化的空间有限；若全市场风险偏好修复与政策工具落地，
微盘/北证/双创有望出现阶段性反弹，中期弹性看增量资金。 
分歧六：11月的"日历效应"这次能否相信？ 历史上11月是A股偏强的月份（2000
年以来26次中18次上涨、均值+1.83%），"大幅回调后的次月"的统计（56.1%胜率）
同样偏正面；但2026年以来日历效应已多次失效（3月、7月、9月均未遵循季节性）。
我们的回答：日历效应只提供"弱概率支撑"，不构成方向判断；11月的方向由"政策维稳
的落地强度"与"AI信用冲击的减弱速度"共同决定。

5.   2026年11月展望 
5.1.   量化模型：权益综合信号转为-2 
"权益综合信号"月频量化择时模型以基本面、资金面、国际面、估值面、技术面五
维度打分合成综合信号（正看多、负看空、0中性），据以判断万得全A月度方向，样
本自2016年1月起、数据截至2026年10月31日。

所对应的"低位约束"也是历史少见，模型信号与结构数据的组合指向"下行空间受限、但
趋势反转未至"——即：负收益的概率高、但"负收益的幅度"未必重复历史均值，短期更
应理解为“下行空间受限、超跌修复概率上升”；中期趋势反转仍需成交与AI信用信
号确认。负收益概率虽高，但短期修复窗口正在打开。

5.2.   宽基方向：五维框架 
五个维度并非平行，其中 F2（政策+海外）主导方向，F1 看下行空间，F3 看上行
斜率，F4、F5 分别提供概率参考与最终情景加权。落到2026年11月： 
F1 风险收益结构：整体低位、防御端已不再便宜 
图1：万得全 A指数风险趋势模型（2026/10/30）

数据来源：Wind，东吴证券研究所

2026年10月30日，万得全A风险度-1.17（自成立以来2.7%分位），为2000年
以来历史第11低位区域、低于2016年以来任何一次月末水平（2024年9月20日低点
为-0.55、2022年10月为-0.46）。历史上万得全A风险度≤0的交易日共223天，近五
次典型出现时点及此后表现：2024年9月20日（未来1个月+28.4%、3个月+32.2%）、
2023年6月12日（+0.4%、-1.2%）、2022年10月17日（+0.7%、+3.1%）、2018年
7月9日（-5.1%、-6.9%）、2016年2月15日（+1.6%、+3.5%）。"低风险度"是赔率信
号而非胜率信号：五次中1个月后4次为正但分化较大（+28.4%至-5.1%），3个月后3
次为正——低位意味着"向下的空间有限、向上的路径依赖催化"，与正文分歧二的"政策
底≠市场底"相互印证。 
结构上分两端：超跌端（创业板指-6.06、科创50-7.42、中证500-3.81，分位均处自
成立以来1%-2%较低区间）具备厚安全垫；防御端（红利指数74.29、66.3%分位；银行

### Page 15

15 / 23 
股月内创新高）拥挤度已抬升，"便宜"的成分下降。 
结论：整体风险度较低构成11月"下行空间受限"的核心依据，但方向性机会需要
超跌端出现"催化触发"，防御端的相对优势依赖风险偏好继续收缩。 
F2 主导逻辑边际变化：政策维稳"密度上升" vs 外部风险"两段式" 
强化项（国内）：五中全会（10月26日-29日）后金融管理部门密集表态，证监会
"全力稳市场稳预期"；央行公开市场"换锚"（11月2日起隔夜逆回购常态化、7天期逆
回购调整为利率招标）改善短端利率调控的灵活性；全国人大常委会（11月2日-6日）
审议金融法草案二审稿；政策工具箱在"稳市场"方向的可用性上升。 
加重项（海外，选举前）：美国中期选举前的观望效应（资金降低风险承担），美
股AI链的信用冲击（英特尔、亚马逊事件）仍待减弱，费城半导体指数10月下跌逾15%
后尚未出现趋势性企稳。 
加重项（海外，选举后）：美方被报道筹备选举后恢复对伊朗打击（最快11月 4
日）、卡塔尔斡旋结果待定；12月FOMC（12月8日-9日）加息悬念未解（10月FOMC
两名票委主张加息、Logan称至少还需两次加息）。 
缓和项：美债10年期收益率已回落至4.929%（"利率逆风"边际减弱）；油价在斡
旋进展下出现过单日逾2%的回落（若选举后局势降温，或触发通胀-利率预期的连锁缓
和）；美元在月内冲高后回落。 
结论：11月短期更可能呈现政策维稳与超跌修复主导的震荡上行/修复格局，中期
取决于中选后地缘路径。外部风险两段式意味着短期修复可期、中期波动仍在。11月第
一周的美国中期选举与随后的地缘窗口是本轮宏观定价的"分水岭事件"。 
F3 资金博弈结构：缩量去杠杆的后半程 
2026年10月全市场日均成交16097亿元、较9月的18315亿元缩量12.1%，月末
五个交易日成交在14264亿-19729亿元区间波动、无持续放量迹象；两市融资余额由9
月末的25324亿元降至10月29日的23492亿元（-1833亿元），降幅为2026年以来最
大，且月末仍未出现明确的止跌形态。另一方面，流动性总量维持宽松：央行10月30
日当周公开市场净投放14110亿元（创三个半月单周新高），叠加11月2日起隔夜逆
回购常态化操作的"换锚"，资金利率的波动率预计降低。 
结论：11月资金面处于杠杆去化后半程，短期增量资金系统性入场条件尚不充分，
但政策工具与流动性‘换锚’有望带来边际回流；行情短期以结构修复为主，中期若两融
止跌、成交回升，修复范围有望扩散。

规律显示"大幅回调后的次月偏反弹"（全样本胜率56.1%、2020年以来66.7%），11
月本身也是历史偏强月份（69%胜率）。但需警惕两点：其一，"大幅回调后的次月"的
均值仅+0.30%（中位数+1.08%），且样本中不乏继续回调的案例（2022年3月-7.5%
后次月-9.5%、2022年9月-7.6%后次月-2.5%）；其二，2026年以来日历效应已多次失
效（2026年3月-8.7%后次月+8.7%兑现反弹， 但2026年7月-13.1%后的8月仅+5.4%、
9月-5.3%后的10月更是-12.4%）。综合看，日历效应对11月构成"弱概率支撑"，可
作为"下行空间受限"的辅助证据。

5.4.   行业观察（产业链基本面五条线索） 
线索一：能源与通胀链（石油石化+煤炭+农业）——布伦特原油10月上涨13.6%
（地缘溢价主导），石油石化板块+3.34%、煤炭+1.29%为仅有的两个正收益周期方向。
11月的核心变量是"中选后地缘路径"：若打击落地，油价与炼化景气的交易延续；若斡
旋达成降温，需防溢价回吐。该线索是当前"基本面与事件驱动共振度"最高的方向。 
线索二：防御/红利链（银行+食品饮料+公用事业+交通运输）——类债属性在"缩量
+去杠杆"环境中占优，但拥挤度抬升（红利指数66.3%分位）与"高位滞涨"微观特征提
示：防御的方向价值仍在、上行性价比下降。三季报验证：银行净利润增速企稳（六大
行前三季合计净赚约1.14万亿元）、食品饮料龙头业绩平稳。 
线索三：超跌成长链（电子+通信+计算机）——电子行业三季报净利润同比增长1.0
倍、通信设备（光模块链）业绩兑现度高（中际旭创前三季归母净利润同比+211.81%），
"业绩与股价背离"意味着一旦AI叙事企稳，该方向可能具备较大的修复弹性；11月的
观察点是费城半导体指数的企稳形态与存储链价格预期（涨价逻辑是否重启）。

### Page 18

18 / 23 
线索四：政策托底链（地产+基建+消费）——前三季度地产投资-20.6%显示基本面
压力仍在；政策端（房贷贴息、现房销售改革扩围至7城）以"精准托底"为主，总量弹
性有限。人大常委会（11月2日-6日）金融法二审与"十五五"开局项目落地节奏是政策
链的观察窗口。该线索的参与价值集中于"政策表态期"的博弈性机会，而非趋势性机会。 
线索五：北交所链（北证50+专精特新）——北交所354家公司三季报披露收官、
37家净利翻倍；北证50月内-14.80%后估值回落（中证指数公司口径市盈率33.66倍），
但增量资金（北证50ETF进度）落地前，修复更可能滞后于主板。该线索属"赔率型"方
向：较低的成交占比与风险度意味着若增量工具落地并带来实际资金流入，相关板块的
修复弹性值得关注。

6.   核心结论与总结 
6.1.   复盘结论

### Page 19

19 / 23 
2026年10月A股的核心特征是"全球AI信用重定价"与"内部拥挤端去化"的共振
深调：万得全A单月-12.44%（2016年1月以来第三大单月跌幅），创业板指-23.02%、
科创50-24.13%（均为历史第二大单月跌幅），通信、电子领跌（-30.23%、-25.70%），
仅防御与能源录得正收益（食品饮料+5.31%、银行+4.84%、石油石化+3.34%）。与9月
加息相关预期带来的估值压力不同，10月美债收益率回落了约35.6个基点、A股却加
速下跌——主导矛盾已从"无风险利率"切换为"AI叙事的信用审视+内部筹码结构"：全
月缩量12.1%（日均成交16097亿元）、两市融资余额下降1833亿元，量价形态属"存
量资金被动降杠杆"而非"恐慌式出逃"。月末（10月26日-29日）五中全会召开、金融
管理部门密集释放"稳市场"信号，指数却收于月内低点——政策底的"信号"与市场底的
"筹码"之间仍有时滞。 
6.2.   展望结论 
2026年11月是"政策维稳+超跌约束"与"外部风险窗口+AI信用冲击"的拉锯，基准
判断为短期震荡修复，中期磨底确认：下有风险度低位（万得全A 2.7%分位）与政策底
封住深度回调空间，上有中选后地缘窗口、12月FOMC悬念与去杠杆未完成约束反弹
斜率。与10月初"顶下移"的修正不同，11月展望的核心修正是"底渐近"：修复的启动需
要"成交回升+AI链企稳"的双确认（详见5.5量化跟踪指标），而模型11月信号-2（历
史上-2样本平均收益-2.09%）提示趋势层面的谨慎仍未解除。结构上，基准情景的占优
方向仍为防御（银行/食品饮料/公用事业）与能源（石油石化/煤炭），超跌成长（电子
/通信）具备赔率、等催化；微盘与北证50属"去化后半程"，反弹弹性弱于主板。 
6.3.   核心预期差清单 
表14：核心预期差清单（2026年11月） 
市场共识 我们的不同判断 
"美债回落将带动A股修复" 
10月数据显示：美债下行35.6bp、A股延续回调；修复条件应换锚为"AI信用冲击
减弱+杠杆去化完成"，美债阈值参考权重下调 
"五中全会维稳信号意味着
政策强刺激" 
全会主题为从严治党与《决定》，金融管理部门的"稳市场"表述属预期管理与制度
性建设，增量刺激需等人大常委会（11月2日-6日）与后续政策窗口确认 
"跌深了就该反弹" 
双创风险度为历史较低分位（1.5%-1.8%分位）提供赔率；胜率确认需要费半企稳+
成交承接。 
"防御/红利已拥挤，行情将
切向成长" 
防御拥挤度抬升属实（66.3%分位），但在"缩量+去杠杆+地缘不确定"的组合下，防
御的相对优势依赖风险偏好收缩；切换的确认信号是风险度回升与成交放量。 
"油价见顶、通胀交易结束" 
油价的地缘溢价由中选后路径决定：打击落地则溢价上冲、斡旋达成或将回吐；通
胀链的定价是"事件驱动"而非"趋势驱动"，需结合11月4日的时间窗口审慎跟踪 
"北证50与微盘已跌透" 
风险度回落至中位但动量较低、月内收于低点；去拥挤的"量"完成大半。反弹的触
发依赖增量资金工具（北证50ETF）与全市场风险偏好修复

6.4.   关键跟踪变量

### Page 20

20 / 23 
2026年11月判断的证伪与验证，取决于以下几个可跟踪信号：① 美国中期选举
（11月3日）及选举后的地缘窗口（11月4日前后）——油价与全球风险偏好的分水
岭；② AI信用冲击的减弱信号——费城半导体指数企稳形态、科技巨头信用利差与资
本开支指引变化；③ 两融余额与成交额——去杠杆进程（止跌）与增量资金（1.8万亿
元）的双观察；④ 全国人大常委会（11月2日-6日）——金融法二审与"十五五"开局
政策信号；⑤ 央行"换锚"（11月2日起）后的短端利率与流动性分布——隔夜逆回购
常态化对资金面的实际影响；⑥ 美联储12月会议（12月8日-9日）预期路径——CME
加息概率与美债反应；⑦ 万得全A风险度与微盘短期动量——低位修复与去拥挤结束
的量化确认。 
6.5.   上期观点回顾与验证 
上期《A股9月复盘与10月展望--缩量调整中的结构性分化，与政策托底的十字路
口》对2026年10月作出多项前瞻判断，现以2026年10月实际数据完整回验。 
判断一（10月行情情景）：基准"震荡修复、非单边"，明确否定"延续下跌"。 2026
年10月万得全A实际下跌12.44%（三段式：上旬-4.41%、中旬-2.71%、下旬-5.85%），
为2016年1月以来第三大单月跌幅。判定：错。 偏离归因：低估了"全球AI信用重定
价"的传导强度与高融资余额下"拥挤端集中去化"的负反馈；当时对"缩量制约上行"的判
断方向正确，但把下行风险主要挂在"海外高利率"（实际美债回落）与"12月日历效应"
上，错置了风险来源。可复用的教训：当估值、筹码与叙事三者同时存在脆弱性时，应
按"最坏情景路径"而非"基准路径"准备。 
判断二（结构判断）："微盘股相对承压、超跌成长相对占优"。 2026年10月：万
得微盘股指数-14.74%（跑输万得全A约2.3个百分点）——承压兑现； 创业板指-23.02%、
科创50-24.13%（分别跑输万得全A约10.6、11.7个百分点）——"超跌成长占优"被完
全反向验证。判定：部分对（微盘承压正确、超跌成长占优错误）。 教训："超跌"不是
发动条件，"超跌+催化+筹码出清"才是；在外部信用冲击未减弱时，跌幅越深的方向反
而可能因筹码结构（融资盘、主题基金集中）而继续跑输。 
判断三（行业方向）："政策托底链、超跌修复链占优、资源品承压"。 2026年10
月：政策托底链中银行+4.84%、食品饮料+5.31%（边际上由"托底链"实际演绎为"防御链
"，方向正确）；超跌修复链（电子-25.70%、电力设备-15.88%）方向错误；资源品整体
并未承压——石油石化+3.34%、煤炭+1.29%（能源链走强），仅有色金属-14.62%（工
业金属承压），内部严重分化。判定：部分对（防御方向正确、超跌修复与"资源品承压
"总体错误）。 教训：资源品的定价要看"能源（地缘溢价）"与"工业金属（全球需求）
"的拆分，不能作为整体判断。 
判断四（量化模型）：2026年10月综合信号-1（模型方向判断偏弱）。 万得全A 
10月下跌12.44%，方向兑现、且为-1信号历史上幅度最大的兑现之一。判定：对。 且
本次验证了"The model > 结构直觉"的规律：当模型信号与"政策底、风险度低位"等结构

### Page 21

21 / 23 
信号冲突时，模型侧再次胜出（连续第4个月方向正确）。 
判断五（微盘择时）：2026年10月9日首次触发"局部顶左侧信号"（短期动量由
62.94降至35.69），提示微盘拥挤风险。 此后微盘股于10月15日-16日转向"局部顶
右侧信号"、10月19日起进入"局部顶，右侧信号2"持续状态，全月下跌14.74%、风险
度由106.1回落至73.9。判定：对，且信号链条完整兑现（左侧→右侧→回撤）。 可复
用经验："高拥挤+动量背离（风险度新高而短期动量骤降）"的顶部信号，在外部冲击来
临时兑现速度较快。 
判断六（情景框架）：基准"震荡承压、6000-6400区间"、悲观"下行至5800附近"。 
实际走势跌破基准区间下沿并突破悲观情景的指示位（收于5425.00点）。判定：基准
错、悲观方向对但幅度被突破。 教训：当情景框架的"基准"依赖某一条件（此处为"地
缘不实质升级"）而该条件在月内即被打破（霍尔木兹油轮事件、美方筹备选举后打击）
时，应即时切换至悲观路径跟踪，而非等待月末复盘。 
判断七（风格推演）：红利/价值"由占优转为震荡"、大盘成长"超跌待修复"、微盘
"谨慎"、资源"震荡"。 实际：红利+2.33%、银行+4.84%（防御持续占优，"转震荡"判断
偏保守）；大盘成长方向判断落空、继续回调（同期创业板指-23.02%）；微盘"谨慎"正
确；能源强于"震荡"预期。判定：部分对（微盘、能源判断正确；防御与成长方向偏差）。 
判断八（跟踪阈值）："美债10年期跌破5.0%则压制缓和、成长修复弹性打开"。 
条件实际触发（10月下旬美债下行至4.929%），但A股并未修复、成长加速下跌。判
定：阈值机制失效。 这是本期最重要的方法论修正：A股修复条件的跟踪锚应从"美债
利率"切换为"AI信用冲击减弱（费半/信用利差）+内部杠杆去化（两融/成交）"，美债
与美元降级为背景变量。 
判断九（风险提示）：微盘拥挤度与局部顶风险、地缘冲突升级风险、政策效果不
及预期风险。 三项风险中，微盘与地缘两项均兑现（微盘-14.74%；霍尔木兹油轮事件、
美方筹备选举后打击），政策链风险亦兑现（地产-15.01%）；唯"美联储加息风险"在10
月未兑现（FOMC按兵不动、但12月悬念未解）。判定：风险清单命中率高，但"风险
清单命中"未能转化为"方向判断正确"——风险提示与情景框架之间需要更刚性的联动
机制（本期已在本报告F5情景表中强化，见"触发条件"列）。

7.   风险提示 
1) 美国中期选举后地缘升级风险（概率中、影响高） 
美方被报道筹备在选举后恢复对伊朗的打击（最快11月4日），若落地，原油与
能源价格上行将强化全球再通胀、推高利率中枢并压制风险偏好。跟踪预警指标为霍尔
木兹海峡通航状态、布伦特原油价格（上破125美元则升级确认）与卡塔尔斡旋进展。

22 / 23 
2) 美联储12月加息风险（概率中、影响高） 
10月FOMC按兵不动但两名票委主张加息、12月悬念未解； 若加息超出市场预期，
美债收益率与美元可能面临上行压力。跟踪预警指标为CME加息概率（升破50%则偏
空）与美债10年期收益率（上破5.2%则压制加剧）。 
3) AI信用冲击延续风险（概率中、影响高） 
英特尔、亚马逊等事件显示AI叙事正从"景气定价"转向"信用与回报审视"，若再出
现巨头评级下调、融资受阻或资本开支指引大幅收缩，全球科技股与A股AI链将二次
承压。跟踪预警指标为费城半导体指数、科技巨头信用违约互换报价与资本开支指引。 
4) 去杠杆负反馈风险（概率中、影响中）

双创等拥挤端可能再度脉冲式下跌。跟踪预警指标为两融余额日度变化、微盘股短期动
量与成交结构。 
5) 政策维稳不及预期风险（概率中、影响中） 
五中全会后金融管理部门的"稳市场"表述若缺乏后续增量工具（流动性、资本市场
制度），政策底的预期支撑将衰减。跟踪预警指标为人大常委会审议内容与证监会后续
举措、成交额变化。 
6) 数据口径风险（概率低、影响低） 
行业/风格/指数涨跌为原始数据2026年9月30日至2026年10月30日实算；风险
度为因子数据 TR（各指数自成立以来分位）；美债收益率为美国财政部日度收盘口径
（2026年10月30日：10年期4.929%）；全球资产表现部分采用市场收盘与媒体转述
口径（已在表5标注）。海外市场与事件信息以公开报道为准，单一来源表述已标注。

东吴证券投资评级标准 
投资评级基于分析师对报告发布日后 6至 12个月内行业或公司回报潜力相对基准表现
的预期（A 股市场基准为沪深 300 指数，香港市场基准为恒生指数，美国市场基准为标普 
500 指数，新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转
让标的），北交所基准指数为北证 50指数），具体如下： 
公司投资评级： 
买入：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在 15%以上； 
增持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于 5%与 15%之间； 
中性：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-15%与-5%之间； 
卖出：预期未来 6个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在-15%以下。 
行业投资评级： 
增持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对强于基准 5%以上； 
中性： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对基准-5%与 5%； 
减持： 预期未来 6个月内，行业指数相对弱于基准 5%以上。 
我们在此提醒您，不同证券研究机构采用不同的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相
对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议。投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定应当充分考虑自身
特定状况，如具体投资目的、财务状况以及特定需求等，并完整理解和使用本报告内容，不
应视本报告为做出投资决策的唯一因素。

MACRO STRATEGY
The Five Shocks of 2026—And the Extraordinary Resilience of the U.S. Economy
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
This is the year the consensus got mugged.
Rewind to the start of the year. The market consensus was that the U.S. economy was
set to slow. The Fed was set to cut interest rates. Corporate earnings were set to grow—

but not explode. Energy prices would remain tame. And the odds looked good that the
Republicans would control the House, the Senate, or both, following the midterm election.
Nine months on, the landscape looks starkly different. Defined by five shocks—energy,
interest rates, earnings, growth, and politics—2026 looks nothing like the year investors
thought they were buying on January 1.
Shock 1: Diesel Matters. While global oil prices have moved higher since the start of

the U.S./Iran war on February 28, oil prices have yet to surpass their previous record high
because the world has released some 400 million barrels from strategic reserves this year,
China has curbed its demand for oil imports, and the U.S. has goosed its energy exports to
record highs.
But here is the problem: While gasoline powers the American consumer, diesel powers the
American economy—and diesel prices are now sitting at record highs. Diesel supplies have

been hammered by wars in the Middle East and Ukraine that have sharply reduced refining
capacity. According to the International Energy Agency, by August, exports of diesel from
refiners in the Middle East had fallen by a quarter from prewar levels. Meanwhile, Ukraine
has hit 24 of Russia’s 34 largest refineries, reducing supplies from one of the world’s
largest diesel exporters. The upshot: U.S. diesel prices have soared from $3.55 a gallon
at the start of the year to roughly $6.52 a gallon. While crude oil trades for around $100

a barrel, the retail equivalent of a barrel of diesel now costs more than $260. The diesel
shock of 2026 was the last thing investors saw coming—and remains a key impediment to
structurally lower inflation.
Shock 2: From Interest Rate Cuts to Rate Hikes. Perhaps no other consensus view
entering 2026 has been turned more dramatically on its head than expectations for the
Fed. Heading into 2026, markets were pricing in rate cuts on expectations of a cooling

labor market, moderating inflation, and slower economic growth. The Fed easing cycle was
expected to continue in 2026. In January 2026, futures showed a roughly 50% chance of a
cut by March.
Expectations, however, flipped in late February with the start of the U.S./Iran war. The
Middle East energy shock reignited inflationary pressures, which, combined with stronger-
than-expected U.S. economic growth and a tightening labor market, have demolished the

odds of easier money in 2026. Short- and long-term Treasury yields rose sharply last week
in the wake of the S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index (PMI)
which pointed to the strongest business expansion in more than five years and the fastest
employment growth in over four years, while also reinforcing expectations that the Fed
will maintain a restrictive policy stance for longer. Markets were expecting two to three
quarter-point rate cuts in 2026; instead, the Fed has already raised rates once this year and

is expected to keep raising rates over the near term.
Shock 3: The Earnings Boom No One Saw Coming. Not all the shocks of 2026 are
negative. Indeed, stronger-than-expected corporate earnings have been among the most
positive factors of this year. Corporate America entered the year facing questions about
tariffs, AI-related capital expenditure spending, the health of the U.S. consumer, operating
leverage and the U.S. dollar outlook. At the start of the year, Wall Street was expecting

roughly $290 of S&P 500 earnings per share for 2026, per Bloomberg estimates. But by
September 21, the estimate had risen by $50, to $340 per share.
Investment Implications
While some of these shocks may
contribute to periods of market
volatility, the combination of strong
earnings growth, robust domestic
demand, and ongoing capital
investment suggests that the
fundamental backdrop for Equities
remains constructive.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Earnings have been upgraded and underpinned by several factors, including robust final 
domestic demand in the U.S., a pickup in global growth, AI-related capital spending, 
productivity gains, and enhanced operating leverage. All these variables have helped deliver 
an earnings “shock” to Wall Street. And as an aside, yes, headline profit growth has been 
inflated by strong technology earnings and one-off investment gains from Amazon and 
Alphabet. That said, the S&P 500’s earnings story remains extraordinary. According to 
FactSet, the 493 companies excluding the Magnificent 7 1 
posted 31.8% earnings growth in 
Q2, the strongest level since late 2021.

Shock 4: An Economy with more Horsepower than the Headlines Suggest. That 
corporate profits have exceeded expectations this ye
ar is due in no small part to an economy 
that continues to surprise to the upside. The Fed was supposed to cut rates this year to 
keep the economy humming and prevent it from losing steam. But rather than limp through 
2026, the economy has leaped ahead. Yes, real gross domestic product (GDP) growth of 
2.1% in Q1 and 1.5% in Q2 are hardly blockbuster annualized rates of growth. However, the 
figures belie the underlying strength of the U.S. economy, with real final domestic demand

(which strips out trade) running at a 4.2% annual rate in Q2. According to the latest reading 
of the Atlanta Fed GDP tracker, Q3 real GDP grow
th is running at a red-hot rate of 5.1%. 
Slowdown? What slowdown?
Resilient consumer spending, robust capital investment, record leve
ls of new business 
formation, and strong foreign direct investment inflows have combined to stoke growth 
in the $32 trillion behemoth called the U.S. economy—the most resilient and dynamic 
economy in the world.
Shock 5: The Unexpected Washington Reset. The last shock is still in the making, but 
given current trends, the odds are rising that the Democrats could control both the House 
and Senate follow
ing the midterm elections. To this point, in January, and per Kalshi, the 
Democrats had a 71% shot at the House and an 18% chance of taking the Senate. Fast-
forward to today, and the odds are a 90% chance that the Democrats take the House and a 
60% chance that they take the Senate.
How all of this plays out remains to be seen—there are some 36 days before the November 
vote, and anything can happen. The market base case remains that the Democrats take the 
House, and the Senate is a toss-up—or too close to call. But with the Democrats expected to 
control at least one chamber of Congress, inv
estors can expect more volatility and uncertainty 
about negotiations ov
er the budget, debt ceiling, defense spending and AI regulation. Another 
political wildcard to watch: the changing dynamics within the Democratic Party.
The Biggest Shock of All for 2026? Pe
rhaps it’s not $6 diesel, rising interest rates, 
solid growth, booming profits or shifting politics. Rather, it’s the ability of the U.S. 
economy to absorb the unexpected, not buckle under stress, and continue generating  wealth 
for investors. To wit, the S&P 500 is on pace for its fourth
 consecutive year of double-digit 
returns.

MARKET VIEW
2026 Has, So Far, Defied the Traditional Midterm Year Playbook
Jordy Fuentes, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Current Landscape. To no surprise, market volatility persisted in 2026. Performance initially
tracked historical midterm election patterns before diverging meaningfully as the year
progressed, though there are still three months to go. Several items remain at hand as U.S.

voters head to the polls in November, including geopolitical and foreign policy uncertainty,
energy driven inflation pressures, affordability concerns, U.S. fiscal trajectory, and rapid
advancements in AI. Regardless of the eventual political outcome, history suggests that
markets and the economy can perform well across different configurations of government.
In our view, markets continue to be anchored by strong fundamentals and encouraging
seasonal trends.

To Be Addressed. U.S. domestic and foreign policy remain in focus as the global reorder,
tit-for-tat tariffs, and geopolitical conflicts spanning the Middle East to Eastern Europe have
added layers of uncertainty to the U.S. economic outlook.
At the same time, persistent geopolitical flare-ups have pushed oil prices materially
higher, adding to inflation pressures and keeping them above the Fed’s 2% target.
Headline Consumer Price Index (CPI) held above 3% year-over-year (YoY) in August, while

its core measure, which excludes food and energy, held at 2.4% YoY. The latest Personal
Consumption Expenditure (PCE), the Fed’s preferred measure of inflation, also remained
above 3% YoY for both its headline and core figures. 2
The U.S. fiscal trajectory is another area of focus. The U.S. budget deficit stands at 5.4% of
GDP , just above this century’s average of 4.6%. Meanwhile, U.S. public sector debt surpassed
the $40 trillion mark for the first time in August, oscillating around 100% of GDP .3 While these

trends warrant close monitoring, we view them as a slow-moving structural headwind rather
than an immediate concern, particularly within a highly innovative and diverse U.S. economy.
AI has emerged as a policy consideration, with debates increasingly centered on both
regulatory safeguards and the economic implications of the expanding infrastructure buildout.
For voters, those concerns extend to affordability, as the significant energy requirements of

new data centers raise questions about potential pressure on electricity costs.
Strap In for The Ride. Leading up to the midterm election, markets tend to experience
greater swings as investors navigate uncertainty surrounding the policy path for the
remainder of the presidential cycle (Exhibit 2A). Since 1930, the S&P 500 has averaged an
intrayear drawdown of 20% in midterm years, with the latest election in 2022 experiencing
a 25% intrayear pullback. This year, however, the S&P 500 largest peak-to-trough drawdown

has been 9%, considerably shallower than its long-term average (Exhibit 2B). Yet while
volatility may rise as election day approaches, 2026 has increasingly defied the traditional
midterm year playbook.
Why 2026 is Different. Despite entering a challenging stretch of the presidential cycle,
U.S. Equities have materially outpaced the typical year two pattern (Exhibit 2C). The S&P
500 experienced its best Q2 in a midterm year since 1938, surging 14.9% compared to an

average decline of 1.6%. From a monthly perspective, September stands out as the weakest
month, though month-to-date performance points to an unusual 1.0% rise. That resilience
follows a 2.6% gain in August, another month in which performance meaningfully exceeded
the historical decline of 0.5% (Exhibit 2D).
Through September, U.S. Equity resilience has also extended beyond headline returns with
both the frequency and magnitude of market pullbacks remaining relatively subdued. Market

corrections of 10% or more have occurred in just 29% of midterm election years since 1930.4
Against this backdrop, the market’s last correction came in spring 2025, while this year has
2 Bloomberg, Bureau of Labor Statistics. Data through August 31, 2026.
3 U.S. Treasury, Bureau of Economic Analysis. Data as of September 21, 2026.
4 Bloomberg, data through December 31, 2022.
Investment Implications
In our view, any related market
weakness could represent a

potential buying opportunity for
long-term investors.

### Page 5

5 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
seen only one 5%+ pullback compared to the average three per year, underscoring relatively
resilient market conditions.5 Year-to-date, the S&P 500 has increased 13%, well above the
average annual return of 3% according to Bloomberg through September 25, and on pace
to mark the strongest midterm election year since 2006. Taken together, 2026 has thus far
defied the traditionally weak midterm year seasonal pattern, reinforcing that every election

cycle is unique and historical trends may be a useful guide to navigate market volatility.
What’s Ahead? Forward performance is increasingly constructive for Equities. In the
post-war period, the S&P 500 rose 100% of the time in the six and 12 months following
elections, averaging 13% and 14%, respectively. 6 Even narrowing the time frame to three
months, markets ended higher 90% of the time. As investors push through the finish line,
the last three months of the year tend to be the strongest with October standing out,

ending higher 67% of the time. On average, Q4 has gained 5.6% since 1930, far surpassing
the preceding three quarters of election years. 7 Greater clarity about the U.S. fiscal policy
path serves as a potential tailwind for investors following the election.
To Take Home. Volatility tends to rise during midterm years, but history suggests that
reacting to short-term political uncertainty has rarely rewarded long-term investors. Over
longer horizons, the data suggests Equity returns and economic strength are shaped less

by election outcomes than by corporate profits, valuations, labor market conditions and
capital investments.

THE TICKER TAPE
What is the bond market telling us?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
We believe the bond market is telling us that the Fed is on track to meeting their dual
mandate of price stability and full employment. More importantly, when examining the
full yield curve the 2-year Treasury yield is currently around 4.88%, which typically is
the most sensitive to the fed funds rate and tends to price in future potential policy

adjustments. At the same time, the 10-year Treasury yield is currently about 5.11%. This
leads us to believe that the market expects the Fed to engineer a couple of more hikes
while the back end of the curve is indicating that economic growth is still healthy.
Taken together, it appears the full curve, which has clearly flattened, believes the Fed is
doing its job, while supply shocks, for obvious reasons, are not leading to higher long-run
inflation. The backup in long-dated yields is growth, not necessarily inflation as indicated

by rising “real” yields. Therefore, we view the drift higher in yields as a more normal sign
than what many have originally suggested. We continue to emphasize higher economic
growth for longer, rising productivity in the medium term, supply shortages to remain in
key areas within the AI space, and shocks in the energy equation. We expect the energy
shocks to ultimately fade away over time, but we do not believe that growth fades
anytime soon. In our view, a more normal bond market pricing in Fed moves at the short

end, and growth at the long end helps financial conditions and economic growth operate
in a more durable manner.
In summary, the front-end of the curve expects 3 to 4 more hikes in the next 18 months
or so. This can be debated obviously based on future data especially short-term energy
supply shocks. Long-end yields are drifting higher based on five main factors below, in
our view.
1. Strong economic growth (both
services and manufacturing)
2. Hawkish Fed

3. Debt and deficit concerns
4. Lower demand for long duration
sovereign debt in general
5. Energy supply shocks
The largest weight being strong economic activity which kick starts portfolio
repositioning particularly as the Fed hikes rates. The repositioning stops once demand
rises again when yields reach attractive levels for the more patient longer term
investment community. The supply shocks ultimately end and debt and deficit worries will
likely remain, but they still take a backseat for now. Longer duration government debt is

becoming more attractive each week even in the face of the concerns above. Be patient

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

S&P 500 Index Price Return is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States. A price return index is a stock
market index that tracks only the price changes and capital gains or losses of its component securities, without including dividends or cash distributions.
S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index is an early monthly economic indicator that estimates overall private-sector business activity by combining manufacturing

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

## Document 314: Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-07).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:16 October 30 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI. Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in
the month, largely owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in domestic and
international holiday travel & accommodation. The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than

anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).

### Page 2

While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation – housing in particular looks to be cooling faster
than expected as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are still showing persistence.
Highlighting this is market services ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator of price
pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail
points to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for another chunky read in Q4.

With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts, and some of the energy risks flagged
then materialising, we continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp rate hike to
4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part
of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding
instead to wait for further information before taking the next step with policy. The decision was made

4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A 25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening
in 2027 (Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems probable; however, each
decision will depend on fuel prices, inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October meeting. Based on prior comments by key
members, the decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed

President Hammack’s dissents in favour of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in
September). Chair Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment (evinced by Q3 GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains

elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual inflation
to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target. This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help
the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December.
Thereafter, the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through
2027; although, in the absence of another shock, we expect them to remain on hold.

The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view on the breadth of growth, the 3.4%
annualised gain for GDP benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household consumption,
12.7% growth in non-residential investment as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment was effectively offset by net exports’ drag
given most of the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is imported. This is not to say that

US firms won’t benefit from the investment once complete, but simply that much of the initial return
goes to the foreign businesses who provide the necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks remain for the consumer, the increase
in nominal spending seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis, consumption flat in the
month following a 0.5% gain in August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the outlook is

### Page 3

Related articles
expected to see household consumption growth average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr
medium-term target as the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline and core
measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy rates unchanged in October. Of note from the
meeting communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks for inflation, but also downside

risks for growth. "While the full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying inflation has
remained broadly stable so far and wages do not show a material response". "Financing conditions
have [also] tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth". In the press conference,
President Lagarde noted that, between the October and December meetings, a significant amount of
new economic information will be released, allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.

Based on the October meeting communications, we continue to expect a rate hike at the December
meeting but believe this will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the spectrum of risks
Europe faces is broad and skewed towards inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that she will step down as ECB President in
February ahead of the scheduled conclusion of her term next October.

.

VIETNAM HAS NOT YET FIXED ITS EXTERNAL LIQUIDITY PROBLEM
, WHICH IS A SOURCE OF VULNERABILITY 
◼

ietnam
 ’s trade balance 
 has

 steadily 
 in 2026. It turned in

19

in the first eight months of 
2026 
 (customs data)

 in the previous year 
 (
amounting to

14

 in the first eight months of 2025
 ).  
This 
 rapid deterioration 
 has resulted notably from the ris
 ing 
import prices of both energy and electronic inputs.  
◼ 
 On the one hand, the current account surplus 
 is 
 decreas
 ing, bu
 t from a comfortable level
 . 
It is projected to narrow 
 to 
2.9
% of GDP in 
2026 from 6.7% in 2025, due to  
the larger energy trade deficit (
 which 
 accounted for

semiconductors/tech goods, and lower export receipts from tourism 
 (about 
 6% of GDP). 
 Furthermore
 , net FDI

strong,  
staying close to 4% of GDP in 2026
 . 
Therefore, 
 Vietnam is  
unlikely to need to incur new foreign debt.  
◼ 
 On the other hand, V
 ietnam
 ’s forex reserves are low and do not provide a solid cushion  
 against a deterioration 
 in

payments
 . Forex reserves amounted to USD8
 8
.
3 
bn at the end of

 , which is

 enough 
to service external debt in 
the

 m 
 but covers less than 3 months of imports of goods 
 and

 f 
 resident capital outflows increase 
(residents’ confidence in the dong 
 may 
be eroded by ri
 s
in
g

 , 
 even

TERM PROSPECTS ARE FAVO

◼ 
 In the medium term, economic growth is expected to remain strong. It is projected at 5.9% per year on average in 2027
 –
2031, 
compared to  
6.2% in the past decade. This is one of the highest growth rates expected in the ASEAN region.  
◼ 
 This  
projection is based on a rather optimistic scenario, as it assumes that:  
i
) FDI continues to flow in, and the manufacturing export sector expands further
 . This 
 means Vietnam’s external accounts 
 continue 
to 
strengthen in the medium term, thanks to continued 
 current account surpluses
 , 
lower resident capital outflows, declining external 
debt and slowly increasing forex reserves
 ; 
ii
) 
D
emographic trends remain 
 favourable
 , even

in the medium

 -

energy and water sectors, boost aggregate productivity (
 i.e.

 -
 productivity FDI firms and low
 -

COUNTRY RISK PROFILE
: STRONG ECONOMIC GROWTH AND LOW PUBLIC 
AND 
FOREIGN DEBT VS. LOW EXTERNAL 
LIQUIDITY AND 
A 
WEAK BANKING SECTOR

◼ 
 Strong factors support 
 Vietnam’s 
 overall country risk  
and economic prospects
 :  
i) 
a 
strong manufacturing export base that is moving up the value chain
 ; 
ii) 
s
olid comparative advantages that continue to attract large FDI inflows
 ; 
iii) 
c
urrent account surpluses and moderate foreign debt
 ; 
iv) 
a 
moderate government debt burden
 ; 
v) 
g
radually improving policy making
 ; 
vi) 
 s
trong medium
 -
 term economic growth prospects, assuming  
 that 
 the authorities will continue to implement structural reforms 
and strengthen productivity in the entire economy.

◼ 
 On the negative side,  
major negative factors continue to fuel some macro
 -
 financial risks:  
i) t
 he excessive domestic debt level of the economy (excluding the government) and fragilities in the banking sector 
 – 
this is a bad 
combination that fuels high credit risks and financial instability risks
 ; 
ii) 
 low forex reserves
 , which 
 have not much strengthened in recent years
 , 
 despite current account surpluses and notably due to 
resident capital flight. Forex reserves do not  
provide good  
protect
ion 
against shocks, while 
 Vietnam 
 is vulnerable to external shocks
 ;  
iii) 
 v
ery high exposure to physical climate risk.  
 C
oastal and river
 -
 delta regions, where population and economic activity are 
concentrated, 
 are 
 highly exposed to sea
 -
 level rise
 s 
and flooding
 .

◼ 
 Non transfer risk and currency risk
 : 
 V
ietnam  
 has enjoyed very 
 favourable  
 external account dynamics in recent years  
 until 2025
 , 
thanks to 
 large FDI inflows
 , 
recurrent 
 current
 -
 account surpluses  
and declining f
 oreign debt (
 which is moderate, at 
 less than  
30% of 
both GDP and exports). 
 This trend 
 has been  
interrupted 
 in 
 2026 
as 
current account surpluses 
 are 
 decreasing
 . Meanwhile
 , 
there is a 
persist
 ent 
 risk of 
 increasing 
 resident capital outflows 
 fuelling  
 some 
 VND 
 depreciation pressure  
 (
w
hereas  
 the potential for foreign 
capital outflows is limited
 )
. 
As 
the forex  
reserve cushion is weak
 , it would 
 not enable the 
 central bank 
 to 
defend the dong for a long 
time 
i
f there is  
severe currency and external liquidity pressure
 . 
However, so far this year, 
 the 
VND/USD spot rate and the reference 
rate 
 (Vietnam has a crawling band FX regime) 
 have depreciated only slightly
 , supported by strong exports and FDI inflows
 .

◼ 
 Sovereign risk
 : 
the government debt burden 
 is moderate and 
 will remain 
 so in the short term, even if the fiscal deficit is projected to 
deteriorate
 . The fiscal deficit could exceed 3% of GDP in 2026 due to support measures in response to the energy shock (estimated at 
less than 1% of GDP in H1 2026), but this will not endanger public
 -
 debt dynamics. Government debt should fall again in 2027 thanks 
to lower deficits and fast GDP growth. Debt was only 30% of GDP in 2025 (vs. 41% in 2019) and interest payments consumed less  
than 5% of fiscal revenue (vs. 7.2% in 2019). The government cover
 s its funding needs mostly in the local bond market, which is 
deepening gradually and not 
 highly 
 exposed to foreign investors. Government debt in foreign currency declined to about 25% of total 
debt in 2025 (vs. 38% in 2019); this is still substantial, but almost all this FC debt 
 is made up  
 of concessional loans from official 
creditors. On the negative front, high credit risks in the state corporate and bank sectors 
 are 
 very large contingent risks for the 
sovereign. Moreover, public investment needs related to climate change and infrastructure development will increase in the 
 medium 
term
.

◼ 
 Systemic credit risk
 : 
Domestic credit to the private sector is too large and still rising (141% of GDP in 2025 vs. 108% in 2019) while 
the banking sector remains poorly capitali
 s
ed. Credit risks and resource misallocation have long been exacerbated by opacity and 
supervision flaws, as well as the use of high credit growth targets to support economic growth (+15% in 2026). In 2022
 –
2023, banks 
faced severe funding stress and asset
 -
 quality deterioration resulting from the property and bond market crisis. The situation has 
recovered gradually since then. In the 
 short term
 , credit risks may increase amid higher energy costs and if  
there is  
a correction in 
the tech cycle. Some banks, especially small private ones, also remain exposed to VND funding risk (whereas banks do not rely  
much 
on 
 foreign
-
 currency 
 financing). Positively, reforms, including stricter capital
 -
 adequacy requirements, continue to be introduced 
gradually 
 in order 
 to improve banks’ creditworthiness (
 the 
 SBV plans to test the removal of credit quotas for the soundest banks 
from 2026).

出，費城半導體指數漲勢尤其明顯 。雖然近

度，日圓走弱並激勵出口類股走勢 。歐股則

供支撐 ，然而全球央行緊縮及公債殖利率走

► 隨人工智慧運算需求持續攀升 ，谷歌、亞馬
遜、臉書、微軟及OpenAI等企業巨頭積極發
展客製化晶片 (ASIC)，藉此提升運算效率並
優化成 本 。根據 凱 基投顧 引述產 業數 據預
估，2027年客製化晶片出貨量將年增 92%至
1,446萬顆 ，首度超 越繪圖處理器 (GPU)的
1,129萬顆 ，顯示AI晶片市場正由 GPU主導
逐步轉向多元化發展。
► 在客製化晶片方面 ，谷歌持續擴大自研張量
處理器 (TPU)的應用與對外供應 ，亞馬遜亦
積極拓展自研AI晶片(Trainium)的雲端運算服
務，預估兩者合計將佔 2027年客製化晶片出
貨量約80%。
► 隨客製 化晶 片需 求 快速增 加 ，相 關晶 片設
計、先 進製 程及 先 進封裝 需求可 望同 步提
升，涉及上述業務發展的台股供應鏈 (註)有機
會受到市場關注。
